- **mcp-server-vet**: Read-only safety inspection of a third-party MCP server BEFORE it is added to a config or trusted. Reads the server's declared tool descriptions, input schemas, scopes, transport, and env/secret surface (not just its README), compares declared behavior against what the tools actually expose, scans for tool-description poisoning and prompt injection, over-broad or undeclared scopes, egress and credential access, config tampering, and hidden Unicode, and returns an add/decline/sandbox verdict for a human to approve.
